# Skills · W · part 47

827 entries, wiki-ingest to wiki-owner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **wiki-ingest**: 用于将外部源文档或对话知识 ingest 到项目 wiki 中。统一委派 kiwi 蒸馏后执行写入，自动维护原始材料副本和反向链接。只要涉及 wiki 摄入、知识归档或文档蒸馏的请求，就请加载此技能。 ([418-teapot/ZooKeeper](https://github.com/418-teapot/ZooKeeper/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Use when adding a new source (file or URL) into the HoReCa knowledge base with synthesis, cross-linking, index updates, and log bookkeeping. ([astra-agency/kb-horeca](https://github.com/astra-agency/kb-horeca/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Ingest a raw source note into an LLM-maintained wiki domain. Triggered by "ingest", "wiki ingest", "add to the wiki", or when processing a vault note into the wiki layer. ([atomicpapa/claude-obsidian-setup](https://github.com/atomicpapa/claude-obsidian-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Ingest a raw source into the wiki - write its source page and integrate it into entity, concept and overview pages. Use when the user adds a file to raw/, pastes or links something to file, or says ingest/process/add this. ([barakgut/llm-wiki](https://github.com/barakgut/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Use this skill whenever you are analyzing a new source to be ingested into the wiki. ([BeckResearchLab/lively-animatronic-llama](https://github.com/BeckResearchLab/lively-animatronic-llama/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Use when ingesting curated source material from raw/ into a wiki based on this template. Covers orientation, source extraction, create-vs-update decisions in practice, page-touch strategy, cross-linking, and index/log update discipline. ([caelaxie/llm-wiki-template](https://github.com/caelaxie/llm-wiki-template/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-ingest**: Digest content into the Wiki knowledge system — source files, inbox items, or session thoughts become organic concept pages with full cross-references. Use when user says "add to wiki", "ingest", "wiki ingest", "capture this to wiki", drops new files in raw/{domain}/sources/, or shares facts/thoughts/feelings to preserve as knowledge. ([Chevis-Zhou/agent-skills](https://github.com/Chevis-Zhou/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Add new knowledge to the wiki — a lesson learned, an edge case hit in production, a documented best practice, or a source document. Routes it to the right domain/category, merges into existing pages before creating new ones, and enforces sourcing and positive-guidance rules. ([choiyounggi/dev-llm-wiki](https://github.com/choiyounggi/dev-llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/skills/ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Ingest current session learnings into the Brain-1 wiki at ~/2nd Brain/2nd Brain/Wiki/. Updates existing pages in place, creates new pages for genuinely new topics, appends to log.md. Use when a significant working session ends with durable learnings or decisions worth keeping. ([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills-library/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Write a research finding, Board session output, or Margot insight back into the Brain-1 wiki. Updates existing pages in place, creates new pages if warranted, appends to log.md, and re-uploads changed files to the Gemini corpus. Use after any research session, Board deliberation, or when the operator says "save this to the wiki". ([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: 将新资料（文章、论文、书籍、GitHub 仓库等）摄入 llm-wiki 知识库，完成"抓取原文 → 写 raw → 抽取实体/概念 → 创建源摘要 → 更新索引和日志"的完整流程。 当用户说"摄入这篇文章"、"ingest 这个 URL"、"把这个加到 wiki"、"收录这个"、"整理一下这篇"、"添加到知识库"， 或者在 llm-wiki 项目里提供一个 URL / 本地文章路径希望纳入知识库时，都应使用此 skill。 即使用户没说 "ingest"，只要在 llm-wiki 项目中明显是在提交一份新资料等待收录，也应主动触发。 ([ConquerTime/llm-wiki](https://github.com/ConquerTime/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Ingests a local file, URL, or pasted text into Herald's long-term memory as a compounding wiki — creates a `sources/` page with takeaways and creates or updates the related `concepts/` and `entities/` pages. Use whenever the user says "ingest this", "remember this article", "add this doc to memory", "save this URL", or hands over a file/link they want the knowledge from — even without the exact word "ingest". ([dbbaskette/herald](https://github.com/dbbaskette/herald/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Compile a source (article, paper, transcript, meeting notes, doc, dataset) into an LLM wiki with provenance: keep an immutable raw copy with a hash, update every existing page the source touches instead of writing one summary page, cite the source at each claim, and label how far each claim can be trusted. Use when adding a new source to a wiki or when asked to read something and add it to the wiki. ([dexio-wiki/llm-wiki-skills](https://github.com/dexio-wiki/llm-wiki-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-ingest**: Process an immutable raw/ source into the jarvis-vault LLM Wiki — the Ingest operation. USE WHEN: the user drops a new source into raw/ and asks to ingest, process, fold in, or summarize it; asks to update the wiki from a source; or asks to compile pending sources into the vault. Covers the ingest worklist, the source→sources/→entities/→concepts/→overview/synthesis/index/log write order, and rebuilding the search index. ([erikschlegel/jarvis-vault](https://github.com/erikschlegel/jarvis-vault/tree/HEAD/plugins/wiki-core/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: 把链接/网页内容收集并织进一个不断生长的本地知识库（LLM Wiki 模式）。当用户发来任意链接（公众号文章、博客、新闻、X/Twitter、论文、视频页等）并想收藏、收集、整理、保存、存起来、记一下、留个底、做成笔记、加进知识库时使用。只要给了链接且意图是留存或整理就用它——哪怕没明说"笔记"。流程：抓正文 → 存不可变原文 → 写摘要页 → 抽概念/实体页并用 [[wikilink]] 互连 → 更新索引。English triggers — collect, save, clip, archive this link/article into my notes, wiki, or knowledge base. ([fiilyai/opennote](https://github.com/fiilyai/opennote/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Ingest a source into the LLM wiki, or re-audit a past ingest. Use when the user asks to ingest a source ("ingest the source at raw/foo.pdf", "/wiki-ingest raw/foo.md", "add this paper to the wiki") or to audit a past ingest ("/wiki-ingest --audit-only &lt;source&gt;", "audit the Williamson ingest", "what did we miss in &lt;source&gt;?"). You compose wiki-ops primitives plus precheck, extraction, and audit judgment. The agent decides the order; common compositions are examples. Does not commit to git; the human reviews first. ([foundry-works/llm-wiki](https://github.com/foundry-works/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/wiki-base/.claude/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Use when ingesting a new source or re-auditing an existing ingest. Compose deterministic `wiki-ops` primitives with human review, extraction, and independent audit. Common flows are examples, not a mandatory pipeline. ([foundry-works/llm-wiki](https://github.com/foundry-works/llm-wiki/tree/HEAD/wiki-base/.agents/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Перенести содержимое PDF (лекция, слайды, конспект, домашка) в Obsidian-заметку `.md` под `wiki/` — дословно, с картинками. Использовать для запросов «перенеси этот PDF», «затащи лекцию в вики», «сделай заметку из PDF», «ingest PDF», «конвертни слайды в Markdown». - **wiki-ingest**: 특정 파일·디렉터리, 또는 최근 git 변경을 gieok Wiki 에 수집하는 경량판 ingest. `/wiki-ingest &lt;path&gt;` 로 경로 지정, `/wiki-ingest` 인수 없음으로 최근 커밋 차분을 대상으로 한다. 일상적인 단발 ingest 용도. 프로젝트 전체를 일괄 투입하고 싶을 때는 `/wiki-ingest-all` 을 사용한다. - **wiki-ingest**: Use when an operation issue asks you to ingest a captured source from `raw/` into the LLM Wiki, or when the user explicitly says "ingest &lt;slug&gt;". - **wiki-ingest**: Use when ingesting a source (transcript, article, summary, meeting note) into an Obsidian-style llm-wiki, or maintaining one. Four modes: ingest (delegate to `summarizing-meetings`, upsert concept/entity pages additively, flag contradictions, update index.md and log.md), lint (orphans, dangling links, missing pages, contradictions), reindex (rebuild index.md from disk, preserve custom sections), query (keyword-find + cite). Triggers: ingest into wiki, register summary, lint, reindex, search notes, compound notes, llm-wiki. ([MatrixFounder/Universal-skills](https://github.com/MatrixFounder/Universal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-ingest) · ★1)
- **wiki-ingest**: Triage what waits in ingest/, and capture what the wiki should learn from: each item goes to the wiki as a source, or to a note in scratchpad/ when it holds work or an idea. Use for ingest, process ingest/, process the inbox, add this file to the wiki, read and file this, batch ingest. Saving part of this conversation is wiki-save; an idea to act on later is a note in scratchpad/ (the almagest skill). - **wiki-orient**: Read an LLM wiki before working in it or answering from it: the schema page, the catalog of page descriptions, recent changes, then a targeted search and the canonical page for the topic. Use at the start of any task that will read or change a shared markdown wiki, and before asking a person to repeat context the wiki may already hold. Require a dry run, use a small batch, preserve semantic grouping, and never treat mass index insertion as proof that the notes are useful. - **wiki-orphan-batch**: Karpathy Lint cycle 後の orphan/missing-index 大量項目を MEMORY index へ batch wikilink 化する自走 skill (= part 142 確立 / wiki_orphan_batch.py thin wrapper)。 ユーザー発話 (= 「orphan batch やって」「missing index 一掃」「memory index 追記」 「wikilink batch」) を検知し scripts/wiki_orphan_batch.py を呼ぶ。 --source orphans|missing_index + --prefixes &lt;csv&gt; で柔軟化。月次分割 MEMORY_&lt;period&gt;.md へ自動振分。 Triggers on: "/wiki-orphan-batch", "orphan batch", "missing index 一掃", "memory index 追記", "wikilink batch", "MEMORY index 拡張", "vault index cleanup".
